mupirocin

CHEBI:7025 EXACT SEEDED antibacterial

What these status tags mean

Curation status describes a workflow state. SEEDED means the record entered through the source-seeding workflow; it does not imply that no later curation occurred. PROPOSED marks an addition awaiting review; REVIEWED records curator sign-off on identity, structure, filing class and producer claims; DEPRECATED retains a superseded or out-of-scope record. These are workflow decisions, not independently verified human review. Agent-assisted contributions may appear in the record history.

Grounding describes the identifier mapping: EXACT uses an ontology identity asserted by the source; MINTED keeps a local identifier when no defensible ontology identity is available; REVIEW_NEEDED flags an exact structure whose ontology identity needs adjudication. The grounding note retains any unresolved alternatives. These tags do not establish the strength of producer or activity evidence.

An α,β-unsaturated ester resulting from the formal condensation of the alcoholic hydroxy group of 9-hydroxynonanoic acid with the carboxy group of (2E)-4-[(2S)-tetrahydro-2H-pyran-2-yl]-3-methylbut-2-enoic acid in which the tetrahydropyranyl ring is substituted at positions 3 and 4 by hydroxy groups and at position 5 by a {(2S,3S)-3-[(2S,3S)-3-hydroxybutan-2-yl]oxiran-2-yl}methyl group. Originally isolated from the Gram-negative bacterium Pseudomonas fluorescens, it is used as a topical antibiotic for the treatment of Gram-positive bacterial infections.

Filing pathway

Polyketides — computed by NPClassifier npclassifier.gnps2.org@2026-09-07, which is why it is pinned rather than recomputed. The gene cluster's asserted class is pks, from MIBiG.

A claim that this taxon makes the compound. The basis says what the evidence addressed — a knockout and a database assertion are not the same claim.

A locus claim and a taxon claim are different questions. Heterologous expression settles the first and leaves the second where the isolation report left it.

Causal graph — biosynthesis 13 nodes, 18 edges

Separate MmpF-KS,MacpA and MacpB genetic phenotypes. Source-assigned products remain ungrounded; no biosynthetic ordering from gene order. Native reference, engineered preparation and study readout remain distinct. Partial primary-main-text curation, not complete pathway reconstitution or full-record sign-off. The exact PAA owner is not the clinical mupirocin mixture.

Whole-cell and purified free-substrate tests remain separate negative contexts. Exact-owner candidate CHEBI:7025 denotes pseudomonic acid A,not the clinical mixture. Native references,experimental preparations and source-assigned readouts are distinct. Substrate9 labels follow the beta-keto assay context; one synthesis sentence instead calls9 beta-hydroxy.
